                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(label, style: TextStyle(color: kSubtext, fontSize: 10)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
        ),
      );
    }

    Widget chip(String label, String value) {
      return Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: kSurface,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          border: Border.all(color: kBorder),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(label, style: TextStyle(color: kSubtext, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
            const SizedBox(height: 2),
            Text(value, style: TextStyle(color: kText, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    return Dialog(
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      insetPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      child: Container(
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 500, maxHeight: 700),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: kBg,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          border: Border.all(color: kBorder),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            // ΓöÇΓöÇ Header ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: kSurface,
                borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(16)),
              ),
              child: Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: kAccent.withAlpha(30),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                      border: Border.all(color: kAccent.withAlpha(80)),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      item.itemCategory.displayLabel,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: kAccent, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const Spacer(),
                  GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                    child: Icon(Icons.close, color: kSubtext, size: 20),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),

            // ΓöÇΓöÇ Body ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
            Expanded(
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      item.name.isNotEmpty ? item.name : 'World Item',
                      style: TextStyle(color: kText, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, height: 1.3),
                    ),
                    if (item.era.isNotEmpty) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(item.era, style: TextStyle(color: kSubtext, fontSize: 13)),
                    ],
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),

                    Row(
                      children: [
                        imageBox(item.imageObverse, 'Obverse'),
                        const SizedBox(width: 12),
                        imageBox(item.imageReverse, 'Reverse'),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),

                    Text('Details', style: TextStyle(color: kSubtext, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, letterSpacing: 0.8)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Wrap(
                      spacing: 8,
                      runSpacing: 8,
                      children: [
                        if (item.country.isNotEmpty)      chip('Country',      item.country),
                        if (item.denomination.isNotEmpty) chip('Denomination', item.denomination),
                        if (item.material.isNotEmpty)     chip('Material',     item.material),
                        if (item.condition.isNotEmpty)    chip('Condition',    item.condition),
                        if (item.purchasePrice != null)
                          chip('Cost', '\$${item.purchasePrice!.toStringAsFixed(2)}'),
                        if (item.estimatedValue != null)
                          chip('Est. Value', '\$${item.estimatedValue!.toStringAsFixed(2)}'),
                        if (item.storageLocation.isNotEmpty)
                          chip('Location', item.storageLocation),
                      ],
                    ),

                    if (item.aiIdentification.isNotEmpty) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Text('AI Identification', style: TextStyle(color: kSubtext, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, letterSpacing: 0.8)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Container(
                        width: double.infinity,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: kSurface,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                          border: Border.all(color: kBorder),
                        ),
                        child: Text(item.aiIdentification, style: TextStyle(color: kText, fontSize: 13, height: 1.4)),
                      ),
                    ],

                    if (item.notes.isNotEmpty) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Text('Notes', style: TextStyle(color: kSubtext, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, letterSpacing: 0.8)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Container(
                        width: double.infinity,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: kSurface,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                          border: Border.all(color: kBorder),
                        ),
                        child: Text(item.notes, style: TextStyle(color: kText, fontSize: 13, height: 1.4)),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            // ΓöÇΓöÇ Footer ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 16),
              child: SizedBox(
                width: double.infinity,
                child: TextButton(
                  onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                  style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                    foregroundColor: kAccent,
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      side: BorderSide(color: kBorder),
                    ),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
                  ),
                  child: const Text('Close'),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ΓöÇΓöÇ Interactive AI Collection Report Modal ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
class _GenerateReportDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userEmail;
  const _GenerateReportDialog({required this.userEmail});

  @override
  State<_GenerateReportDialog> createState() => _GenerateReportDialogState();
}

class _GenerateReportDialogState extends State<_GenerateReportDialog> {
  bool _isLoading = true;
  bool _needsStateSelection = false;
  String? _selectedState;
  String? _error;
  EstateReportResult? _result;

  static const _supportedStates = ['NY', 'NC', 'NJ', 'FL', 'CA', 'TX', 'SC'];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _checkJurisdictionAndStart();
  }

  Future<void> _checkJurisdictionAndStart() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _needsStateSelection = false;
      _error = null;
    });

    try {
      final profile = await EstateProfileService.getProfile(widget.userEmail);
      final jur = profile?.jurisdiction.trim().toUpperCase();
      if (jur != null && jur.isNotEmpty && _supportedStates.contains(jur)) {
        _selectedState = jur;
        await _generate(jur);
      } else {
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() {
            _isLoading = false;
            _needsStateSelection = true;
            _selectedState = null;
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _isLoading = false;
          _needsStateSelection = true;
          _selectedState = null;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _generate(String state) async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _needsStateSelection = false;
      _error = null;
    });

    try {
      final ownerName = widget.userEmail.isNotEmpty ? widget.userEmail.split('@').first : 'Collection Owner';
      final identity = EphemeralReportIdentity(
        ownerLegalName: ownerName,
        reportDate: intl.DateFormat('yyyy-MM-dd').format(DateTime.now()),
        state: state,
      );

      final result = await EstateReportService.generateReport(
        uid: widget.userEmail,
        identity: identity,
        mode: 'living_inventory',
        state: state,
        includePhotos: true,
      ).timeout(
        const Duration(seconds: 60),
        onTimeout: () => throw Exception('Report generation timed out. Please try again.'),
      );

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _result = result;
          _isLoading = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _error = NumistaErrorHelper.toUserFriendly(e, fallback: 'Could not load data. Please try again.');
          _isLoading = false;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;
    const bgCard = Color(0xFF161B27);
    const pink   = Color(0xFFF63366);

    return AlertDialog(
      backgroundColor: bgCard,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
      title: Row(
        children: [
          const Icon(Icons.auto_awesome, color: pink),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Text('AI Collection & Inventory Report', style: TextStyle(color: isDark ? Colors.white : const Color(0xFF0F172A), fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        ],
      ),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 480,
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            if (_isLoading) ...[
              Center(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 24),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      const CircularProgressIndicator(color: pink),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Text('Compiling legal-grade PDF report via Cloud Run...', style: TextStyle(color: isDark ? Colors.white70 : const Color(0xFF5A5C69), fontSize: 13)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ] else if (_needsStateSelection) ...[
              Text(
                'Please select your legal jurisdiction for estate tax and probate calculations:',
                style: TextStyle(color: isDark ? Colors.white70 : const Color(0xFF5A5C69), fontSize: 13),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                // ignore: deprecated_member_use
                value: _selectedState,
                hint: Text('Select state...', style: TextStyle(color: isDark ? Colors.white54 : const Color(0xFF94A3B8))),
                dropdownColor: bgCard,
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'State / Jurisdiction',
                  labelStyle: TextStyle(color: isDark ? Colors.white70 : const Color(0xFF5A5C69)),
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                  enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                    borderSide: BorderSide(color: isDark ? Colors.white24 : Colors.grey.shade400),
                  ),
                ),
                style: TextStyle(color: isDark ? Colors.white : const Color(0xFF0F172A), fontSize: 14),
                items: _supportedStates
                    .map((s) => DropdownMenuItem(value: s, child: Text(s)))
                    .toList(),
                onChanged: (val) {
                  setState(() => _selectedState = val);
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              ElevatedButton.icon(
                onPressed: _selectedState != null
                    ? () => _generate(_selectedState!)
                    : null,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf, size: 18),
                label: const Text('Generate Report'),
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: pink, foregroundColor: Colors.white),
              ),
            ] else if (_error != null) ...[
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: const Color(0x25F63366), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.error_outline, color: pink),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Expanded(child: Text(_error!, style: TextStyle(color: isDark ? Colors.white70 : const Color(0xFF5A5C69), fontSize: 13))),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ] else if (_result != null) ...[
              Text('Your AI Collection Inventory Report has been compiled successfully!', style: TextStyle(color: isDark ? Colors.white70 : const Color(0xFF5A5C69), fontSize: 14)),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                      onPressed: () async {
                        await EstateReportService.openPdf(_result!.pdfBytes, 'Numista_AI_Collection_Report.pdf');
                      },
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf, size: 18),
                      label: const Text('≡ƒôä Open PDF'),
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: pink, foregroundColor: Colors.white),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  IconButton(
                    tooltip: 'Copy Attorney Portal Link',
                    icon: Icon(Icons.link, color: isDark ? Colors.white : const Color(0xFF0F172A)),
                    onPressed: () async {
                      final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
                      await EstateReportService.copyAttorneyLink(widget.userEmail, _result!.reportId);
                      if (mounted) {
                        messenger.showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('Attorney Portal link copied to clipboard!'), backgroundColor: pink));
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        if (_error != null)
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () {
              if (_selectedState != null) {
                _generate(_selectedState!);
              } else {
                _checkJurisdictionAndStart();
              }
            },
            child: const Text('Retry', style: TextStyle(color: pink)),
          ),
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: Text('Close', style: TextStyle(color: isDark ? Colors.white70 : const Color(0xFF5A5C69))),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

// --- Custom Top Scrollbar Track Widget ----------------------------------------
class _TopScrollbarTrackWidget extends StatefulWidget {
  final ScrollController controller;
  final Color accentColor;
  final Color trackColor;

  const _TopScrollbarTrackWidget({
    required this.controller,
    required this.accentColor,
    required this.trackColor,
  });

  @override
  State<_TopScrollbarTrackWidget> createState() => _TopScrollbarTrackWidgetState();
}

class _TopScrollbarTrackWidgetState extends State<_TopScrollbarTrackWidget> {
  double? _dragStartLocalX;
  double? _dragStartScrollOffset;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ListenableBuilder(
      listenable: widget.controller,
      builder: (context, _) {
        // TableView attaches a 2D scroll position. Accessing .position as a
        // 1D ScrollPosition can throw on Flutter web and gray-screen the tab.
        late final double maxExtent;
        late final double viewport;
        late final double offset;
        try {
          if (!widget.controller.hasClients ||
              !widget.controller.position.hasContentDimensions ||
              widget.controller.position.maxScrollExtent <= 0) {
            return const SizedBox.shrink();
          }
          maxExtent = widget.controller.position.maxScrollExtent;
          viewport = widget.controller.position.viewportDimension;
          offset = widget.controller.offset.clamp(0.0, maxExtent);
        } catch (e) {
          debugPrint('Top scrollbar track skipped (2D scroll position): $e');
          return const SizedBox.shrink();
        }

        final double totalContent = maxExtent + viewport;
        final double thumbRatio = (viewport / totalContent).clamp(0.1, 0.9);

        return LayoutBuilder(
          builder: (context, constraints) {
            final double trackWidth = constraints.maxWidth;
            final double thumbWidth = (trackWidth * thumbRatio).clamp(36.0, trackWidth);
            final double maxThumbOffset = trackWidth - thumbWidth;
            final double scrollRatio = maxExtent > 0 ? (offset / maxExtent).clamp(0.0, 1.0) : 0.0;
            final double thumbLeft = scrollRatio * maxThumbOffset;

            return SizedBox(
              height: 24, // 24px hit target for easy mouse / touch interaction
              child: GestureDetector(
                behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                onHorizontalDragStart: (details) {
                  _dragStartLocalX = details.localPosition.dx;
                  _dragStartScrollOffset = widget.controller.offset;
                },
                onHorizontalDragUpdate: (details) {
                  if (maxThumbOffset <= 0 || _dragStartLocalX == null || _dragStartScrollOffset == null) return;
                  final double deltaX = details.localPosition.dx - _dragStartLocalX!;
                  final double scrollDelta = (deltaX / maxThumbOffset) * maxExtent;
                  final double targetOffset = (_dragStartScrollOffset! + scrollDelta).clamp(0.0, maxExtent);
                  widget.controller.jumpTo(targetOffset);
                },
                onHorizontalDragEnd: (_) {
                  _dragStartLocalX = null;
                  _dragStartScrollOffset = null;
                },
                onTapDown: (details) {
                  if (maxThumbOffset <= 0) return;
                  final double clickRatio = (details.localPosition.dx - thumbWidth / 2) / maxThumbOffset;
                  final double targetOffset = (clickRatio * maxExtent).clamp(0.0, maxExtent);
                  widget.controller.animateTo(
                    targetOffset,
                    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
                    curve: Curves.easeOut,
                  );
                },
                child: Center(
                  child: Container(
                    height: 8, // 8px visual track
                    width: trackWidth,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: widget.trackColor,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                    ),
                    child: Stack(
                      children: [
                        Positioned(
                          left: thumbLeft,
                          width: thumbWidth,
                          top: 0,
                          bottom: 0,
                          child: Container(
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: widget.accentColor,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

/// Robust image component for collection card grid.
/// Displays personal scan if available, otherwise queries CoinImageService for reference image.
class _CollectionCardImage extends StatelessWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic> data;
  const _CollectionCardImage({required this.data});

  static String _cleanUrl(dynamic val) {
    if (val == null) return '';
    final s = val.toString().trim();
    if (s.isEmpty || s.startsWith('gs://') || !s.startsWith('http')) return '';
    return s;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final canon = CanonicalMintAssetService.resolveFromMap(data);
    if (canon != null) {
      final canonUrl = canon.reverseUrl.isNotEmpty ? canon.reverseUrl : canon.obverseUrl;
      if (canonUrl.isNotEmpty) {
        return _buildCoinImage(canonUrl);
      }
    }

    final obv = _cleanUrl(data[_F.imageObverse]);
    final rev = _cleanUrl(data[_F.imageReverse]);
    final directUrl = rev.isNotEmpty ? rev : obv;  // Prefer reverse (distinct design)

    if (directUrl.isNotEmpty) {
      return _buildCoinImage(directUrl);
    }

    // Dual-read: PascalCase first, lowercase fallback for unmigrated CSV docs
    String dualRead(String snakeKey, String legacyKey) {
      final s = data[snakeKey]?.toString();
      if (s != null && s.trim().isNotEmpty && s.trim() != 'None' && s.trim() != 'null') return s.trim();
      final l = data[legacyKey]?.toString();
      if (l != null && l.trim().isNotEmpty && l.trim() != 'None' && l.trim() != 'null') return l.trim();
      return '';
    }
    final year = dualRead('year', _F.year).replaceAll(RegExp(r'\.0$'), '');
    final mint = dualRead('mint_mark', _F.mintMark);
    final denom = dualRead('denomination', _F.denomination);
    final series = dualRead('program_series', _F.programSeries);
    final subject = dualRead('theme_subject', _F.themeSubject);

    return FutureBuilder<CoinImageResult>(
      future: CoinImageService.fetchReferenceImages(
        year: year,
        mint: mint.isEmpty ? null : mint,
        denomination: denom.isEmpty ? null : denom,
        series: series.isEmpty ? null : series,
        subject: subject.isEmpty ? null : subject,
      ),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        final ref = snapshot.data;
        // MF-V3-3: When subject is known and reverse is null (steal-guard
        // correctly blocked a generic reverse), show placeholder instead of
        // falling back to generic obverse. Keeps obverse fallback for coins
        // without a subject (e.g. generic Morgan/Peace).
        final refUrl = (ref?.reverseUrl?.isNotEmpty == true)
            ? ref!.reverseUrl!
            : (subject.isEmpty && ref?.obverseUrl?.isNotEmpty == true)
                ? ref!.obverseUrl!
                : '';

        if (refUrl.isNotEmpty) {
          return _buildCoinImage(refUrl);
        }

        if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
          return const Center(
            child: SizedBox(
              width: 24,
              height: 24,
              child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                strokeWidth: 2,
                color: Color(0xFFC9A227),
              ),
            ),
          );
        }

        return const Center(
          child: Icon(Icons.image_not_supported_outlined, size: 36, color: Color(0xFF8B92B4)),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCoinImage(String url) {
    return Center(
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          shape: BoxShape.circle,
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: const Color(0xFFC9A227).withAlpha(40),
              blurRadius: 14,
              spreadRadius: 1,
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: Image.network(
          url,
          fit: BoxFit.contain,
          errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => const Center(
            child: Icon(Icons.image_not_supported_outlined, size: 36, color: Color(0xFF8B92B4)),
          ),
          loadingBuilder: (context, child, progress) {
            if (progress == null) return child;
            return const Center(
              child: SizedBox(
                width: 24,
                height: 24,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                  strokeWidth: 2,
                  color: Color(0xFFC9A227),
                ),
              ),
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


### FILE: numista_mobile/lib/constants/denomination_canon.dart
/// Numista.AI Canonical US Mint Denominations (GI-NOM-01)
/// Authoritative client-side constants for formal US Mint nomenclature.
class DenominationCanon {
  static const String cent          = 'Cent';
  static const String fiveCents     = 'Five Cents';
  static const String dime          = 'Dime';
  static const String quarterDollar = 'Quarter Dollar';
  static const String halfDollar    = 'Half Dollar';
  static const String dollar        = 'Dollar';

  /// Primary active US circulating denominations
  static const List<String> usCirculating = [
    cent,
    fiveCents,
    dime,
    quarterDollar,
    halfDollar,
    dollar,
  ];

  /// Colloquial -> Canonical mapping dictionary
  static const Map<String, String> colloquialMap = {
    'penny': cent,
    'pennies': cent,
    'one cent': cent,
    '1 cent': cent,
    '1c': cent,
    '1┬ó': cent,
    'wheatie': cent,
    'lincoln cent': cent,
    'nickel': fiveCents,
    'nickels': fiveCents,
    'nickles': fiveCents,
    '5 cents': fiveCents,
    '5c': fiveCents,
    '5┬ó': fiveCents,
    'five cent': fiveCents,
    'five cents': fiveCents,
    'jefferson nickel': fiveCents,
    'buffalo nickel': fiveCents,
    '10c': dime,
    '10┬ó': dime,
    'ten cents': dime,
    '10 cents': dime,
    'roosevelt dime': dime,
    'dime': dime,
    'quarter': quarterDollar,
    'quarters': quarterDollar,
    '25c': quarterDollar,
    '25┬ó': quarterDollar,
    '25 cents': quarterDollar,
    'washington quarter': quarterDollar,
    'state quarter': quarterDollar,
    'quarter dollar': quarterDollar,
    'half': halfDollar,
    'halves': halfDollar,
    '50c': halfDollar,
    '50┬ó': halfDollar,
    '50 cents': halfDollar,
    'jfk half': halfDollar,
    'kennedy half': halfDollar,
    'half dollar': halfDollar,
    'dollar coin': dollar,
    '\$1': dollar,
    '1 dollar': dollar,
    'dollar': dollar,
    'silver dollar': dollar,
    'morgan dollar': dollar,
    'peace dollar': dollar,
  };

  /// Normalizes a raw string to canonical form if recognized as US denomination.
  /// Fails open (returns trimmed original) if unknown or foreign.
  static String normalize(String? raw) {
    if (raw == null) return '';
    final trimmed = raw.trim();
    if (trimmed.isEmpty) return '';

    // Strip suffixes like (25C)
    final clean = trimmed.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\s*\(\s*\d+\s*[c┬ó\w\s]*\)', caseSensitive: false), '').trim();
    final lower = clean.toLowerCase();

    if (lower == 'quarter dollar dollar') return quarterDollar;
    if (lower == 'dollar dollar') return dollar;
    if (lower == 'cent cent') return cent;

    return colloquialMap[lower] ?? trimmed;
  }

  /// Autocomplete suggestions for 60+ collectors entering denominations.
  /// e.g. "penn" -> ["Cent (Penny)"], "quart" -> ["Quarter Dollar (Quarter)"]
  static List<String> getSuggestions(String query) {
    final q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (q.isEmpty) return usCirculating;

    final results = <String>{};
    for (final formal in usCirculating) {
      if (formal.toLowerCase().contains(q)) {
        results.add(formal);
      }
    }

    colloquialMap.forEach((alias, formal) {
      if (alias.contains(q) && !results.contains(formal)) {
        results.add('$formal ($alias)');
      }
    });

    return results.toList();
  }
}
### FILE: numista_mobile/lib/services/melt_value_service.dart
// melt_value_service.dart
//
// Standardized Valuation Service for Numista.AI
// Calculates real-time face value and melt value for coins:
//   - Field precedence: Uses explicit numeric fields (denomination_numeric, troy_oz_pure_metal) when available
//   - Fallback normalization: Normalizes truncated/dirty strings ("Five Dollars (Hal..." -> $5.00)
//   - Spot price resilience: Live spot prices with cached fallback and offline indicator support

class MeltValueService {
  // ΓöÇΓöÇ Silver (Ag) troy oz per coin by denomination ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
  static const _ag90 = {
    'dime':        0.07234,   // 2.5g ├ù 90% = 2.25g Ag ├╖ 31.1035
    'quarter':     0.18084,   // 6.25g ├ù 90% = 5.625g Ag ├╖ 31.1035
    'half':        0.36169,   // 12.5g ├ù 90% = 11.25g Ag ├╖ 31.1035
    'half dollar': 0.36169,
    'dollar':      0.77344,   // Morgan / Peace: 26.73g ├ù 90% ├╖ 31.1035
  };

  static const _ag40 = {
    'half':        0.14792,
    'half dollar': 0.14792,
  };

  static const _ag35 = {
    'nickel': 0.05626,
  };

  static const double _silverEagleOz = 1.0;

  // ΓöÇΓöÇ Gold (Au) troy oz per coin by face value ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
  static final _au90ByFaceValue = {
    1.0:  0.04837,   // $1 gold piece
    2.5:  0.12094,   // Quarter Eagle
    3.0:  0.14513,   // $3 gold piece
    5.0:  0.24188,   // Half Eagle
    10.0: 0.48375,   // Eagle
    20.0: 0.96750,   // Double Eagle
  };

  static final _au9167ByFaceValue = {
    5.0:  0.10,
    10.0: 0.25,
    25.0: 0.50,
    50.0: 1.00,
  };

  static const double _buffaloAuOz = 1.0;

  // ΓöÇΓöÇ Unified Face Value Parser ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
  /// Parses denomination string to numerical USD value.
  /// Pre-processes dirty strings by stripping parenthetical notes and ellipses.
  /// Evaluates multi-word terms before generic single tokens.
  static double parseFaceValue(String rawDenom, {int qty = 1}) {
    final count = qty > 0 ? qty : 1;
    if (rawDenom.trim().isEmpty) return 0.0;

    // Normalization pipeline: lowercase, collapse whitespace, strip parenthetical & ellipses
    String s = rawDenom.toLowerCase().trim();
    s = s.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\(.*?\)'), '').trim();
    s = s.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\.{2,}'), '').trim();

    double value = 0.0;

    // 1. High Gold & Commemoratives
    if (s.contains(r'$500') || s.contains('five hundred dollar')) { value = 500.00; }
    else if (s.contains(r'$100') || s.contains('one hundred dollar') || s.contains('hundred dollar')) { value = 100.00; }
    else if (s.contains(r'$50') || s.contains('fifty dollar')) { value = 50.00; }
    else if (s.contains(r'$25') || s.contains('twenty five dollar') || s.contains('twenty-five dollar')) { value = 25.00; }
    else if (s.contains(r'$20') || s.contains('twenty dollar') || s.contains('double eagle')) { value = 20.00; }
    else if (s.contains(r'$10') || s.contains('ten dollar') || (s.contains('eagle') && !s.contains('half') && !s.contains('quarter') && !s.contains('silver'))) { value = 10.00; }
    else if (s.contains(r'$5') || s.contains('five dollar') || s.contains('half eagle')) { value = 5.00; }
    else if (s.contains(r'$3') || s.contains('three dollar')) { value = 3.00; }
    else if (s.contains(r'$2.50') || s.contains(r'$2.5') || s.contains('quarter eagle') || s.contains('two and a half')) { value = 2.50; }
    else if (s.contains(r'$2') || s.contains('two dollar')) { value = 2.00; }

    // 2. Sub-Dollar & Standard Silver/Base Denominations
    else if (s.contains('half dollar') || s.contains('50c') || s.contains('50 cent') || s.contains(r'$0.50') || s.contains(r'$0.5')) { value = 0.50; }
    else if (s.contains('quarter dollar') || s.contains('quarter') || s.contains('25c') || s.contains('25 cent') || s.contains(r'$0.25')) { value = 0.25; }
    else if (s.contains('twenty cent') || s.contains('20c')) { value = 0.20; }
    else if (s.contains('dime') || s.contains('10c') || s.contains('10 cent') || s.contains(r'$0.10') || s.contains(r'$0.1')) { value = 0.10; }
    else if (s.contains('half dime')) { value = 0.05; }
    else if (s.contains('nickel') || s.contains('5c') || s.contains('5 cent') || s.contains(r'$0.05')) { value = 0.05; }
    else if (s.contains('three cent') || s.contains('3c')) { value = 0.03; }
    else if (s.contains('two cent') || s.contains('2c')) { value = 0.02; }
    else if (s.contains('half cent')) { value = 0.005; }
    else if (s.contains('penny') || s.contains('cent') || s.contains('1c') || s.contains('1 cent') || s.contains(r'$0.01')) { value = 0.01; }
    else if (s.contains('dollar') || s.contains(r'$1')) { value = 1.00; }

    // 3. Fallback: plain numeric parse
    else {
      final match = RegExp(r'\d+(?:\.\d+)?').firstMatch(s);
      if (match != null) {
        final parsed = double.tryParse(match.group(0)!) ?? 0.0;
        value = parsed <= 1000.0 ? parsed : 0.0;
      }
    }

    return value * count;
  }

  // ΓöÇΓöÇ Public Melt Value Calculation API ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
  /// Returns melt value in USD, or null for base-metal / non-precious coins.
  static double? compute({
    required String metalContent,
    required String denomination,
    required Map<String, double> spotPrices,
    String programSeries = '',
    String themeSubject = '',
    int qty = 1,
    Map<String, dynamic>? coinData,
  }) {
    final count = qty > 0 ? qty : 1;
    final ag = spotPrices['Silver'] ?? 0.0;
    final au = spotPrices['Gold']   ?? 0.0;

    if (ag == 0.0 && au == 0.0) return null;

    // Field Precedence: Check if backend numeric troy oz field is present
    if (coinData != null) {
      final ozNum = (coinData['troy_oz_pure_metal'] as num?)?.toDouble();
      final isGold = coinData['is_gold'] == true;
      final isSilver = coinData['is_silver'] == true;
      if (ozNum != null && ozNum > 0.0) {
        if (isGold && au > 0) return au * ozNum * count;
        if (isSilver && ag > 0) return ag * ozNum * count;
      }
    }

    final mc     = metalContent.trim().toLowerCase();
    final denom  = denomination.trim().toLowerCase();
    final series = programSeries.trim().toLowerCase();
    final theme  = themeSubject.trim().toLowerCase();
    final combined = '$mc $denom $series $theme';

    // ΓöÇΓöÇ Gold Detection ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
    if (combined.contains('gold') || combined.contains('half eagle') || combined.contains('eagle') || combined.contains('double eagle')) {
      if (combined.contains('99.99') || combined.contains('buffalo')) {
        return au * _buffaloAuOz * count;
      }
      if (combined.contains('91.67') || combined.contains('gold eagle')) {
        final fv = parseFaceValue(denom);
        final oz = _au9167ByFaceValue[fv] ?? 1.0;
        return au * oz * count;
      }
      if (combined.contains('90%') || combined.contains('half eagle') || combined.contains('indian head gold') || combined.contains('pre-1933') || combined.contains('saint-gaudens') || combined.contains('liberty head')) {
        final fv = parseFaceValue(denom);
        final oz = _au90ByFaceValue[fv] ?? 0.24188;
        return au * oz * count;
      }
      if (au > 0) return au * 0.24188 * count;
    }

    // ΓöÇΓöÇ Silver Detection ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
    if (combined.contains('silver') || combined.contains('silver eagle') || combined.contains('peace dollar') || combined.contains('morgan')) {
      if (combined.contains('silver eagle') || combined.contains('99.9') || combined.contains('.999') || combined.contains('fine silver')) {
        return ag * _silverEagleOz * count;
      }
      if (combined.contains('90%') || combined.contains('morgan') || combined.contains('peace') || combined.contains('barber') || combined.contains('walking liberty') || combined.contains('standing liberty') || combined.contains('mercury')) {
        final oz = _matchDenom(_ag90, denom) ?? 0.77344;
        return ag * oz * count;
      }
      if (combined.contains('40%')) {
        final oz = _matchDenom(_ag40, denom) ?? 0.14792;
        return ag * oz * count;
      }
      if (combined.contains('35%') || combined.contains('war nickel')) {
        final oz = _matchDenom(_ag35, denom) ?? 0.05626;
        return ag * oz * count;
      }
      if (ag > 0) return ag * 0.77344 * count;
    }

    return null;
  }

  /// Formatted melt value string. Returns 'N/A' for non-precious coins.
  static String format({
    required String metalContent,
    required String denomination,
    required Map<String, double> spotPrices,
    String programSeries = '',
    String themeSubject = '',
    int qty = 1,
    Map<String, dynamic>? coinData,
  }) {
    final v = compute(
      metalContent: metalContent,
      denomination: denomination,
      spotPrices: spotPrices,
      programSeries: programSeries,
      themeSubject: themeSubject,
      qty: qty,
      coinData: coinData,
    );
    if (v == null) return 'N/A';
    return '\$${v.toStringAsFixed(2)}';
  }

  static double? _matchDenom(Map<String, double> table, String rawDenom) {
    final fv = parseFaceValue(rawDenom);
    if (fv == 1.00 && table.containsKey('dollar')) return table['dollar'];
    if (fv == 0.50 && table.containsKey('half')) return table['half'];
    if (fv == 0.25 && table.containsKey('quarter')) return table['quarter'];
    if (fv == 0.10 && table.containsKey('dime')) return table['dime'];
    if (fv == 0.05 && table.containsKey('nickel')) return table['nickel'];
    return null;
  }
}
### FILE: numista_mobile/lib/services/canonical_mint_asset_service.dart
class CanonicalMintAssetPair {
  final String sku;
  final String obverseUrl;
  final String reverseUrl;
  final String? fallbackObverseUrl;
  final String? fallbackReverseUrl;
  final bool isSet;
  final bool isGenuine2026;
  final bool isRepresentativePreview;

  const CanonicalMintAssetPair({
    required this.sku,
    required this.obverseUrl,
    required this.reverseUrl,
    this.fallbackObverseUrl,
    this.fallbackReverseUrl,
    this.isSet = false,
    this.isGenuine2026 = false,
    this.isRepresentativePreview = false,
  }) : assert(obverseUrl != reverseUrl, 'Obverse and reverse URLs must be distinct');
}

class CanonicalMintAssetService {
  /// MF-B User Upload Guard: Never overwrite user-uploaded photos or microscope scans.
  /// If existing stored images are user uploads (under uploads bucket or /microscope/),
  /// or scan_source is microscope / hardware agent, return true to preserve existing.
  static bool hasUserUploadedPhotos(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    final scanSource = (data['scan_source'] ?? data['source'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
    if (scanSource == 'microscope' || scanSource == 'hardware agent') return true;

    bool isUserUrl(dynamic val) {
      if (val == null) return false;
      final s = val.toString().trim().toLowerCase();
      if (s.isEmpty) return false;
      if (s.contains('/reference_images/') || s.contains('reference_library')) return false;
      return s.contains('/microscope/') ||
             s.contains('studio-9101802118-8c9a8-uploads') ||
             s.contains('numista-uploads-studio-') ||
             s.contains('/users/') ||
             s.contains('/user_uploads/') ||
             s.contains('user_upload');
    }

    return isUserUrl(data['image_url_obverse']) ||
           isUserUrl(data['image_url_reverse']) ||
           isUserUrl(data['imageObverse']) ||
           isUserUrl(data['imageReverse']) ||
           isUserUrl(data['stored_obv']) ||
           isUserUrl(data['stored_rev']);
  }

  static const String _base =
      'https://storage.googleapis.com/numista-reference-library/reference_library';
  static const String _mintBase = '$_base/us_mint_official';
  static const String _wikiBase = '$_base/wikimedia_uscoin';

  static const List<String> _knownSkus = <String>[
    '26EA',
    '26XE',
    '26XL',
    '26XH',
    '26XF',
    '26RJ',
    '26GC',
    '26SQRP',
    '26RG',
    '23RG',
    '26RH',
    '23RH',
  ];

  static String _normYear(Object? y) {
    final s = (y ?? '').toString().trim();
    final m = RegExp(r'^(\d{4})(?:\.0+)?$').firstMatch(s);
    return m != null ? m.group(1)! : s;
  }

  static String extractSku(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    for (final k in const <String>[
      'mint_product_id',
      'sku',
      'product_code',
      'item_number',
      'Retailer Item No.',
      'retailer_item_no',
      'retailer_item_num',
    ]) {
      final val = (data[k] ?? '').toString().trim().toUpperCase();
      if (_knownSkus.contains(val)) return val;
    }
    for (final k in const <String>[
      'Personal Notes',
      'personal_notes',
      'title',
      'name',
      'Theme/Subject',
      'Program/Series',
      'Program / Series',
      'program_series',
    ]) {
      final txt = (data[k] ?? '').toString().toUpperCase();
      final tokens = txt.replaceAll('#', ' ').replaceAll('-', ' ').split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
      for (final candidate in _knownSkus) {
        if (tokens.contains(candidate)) return candidate;
      }
    }
    final year = _normYear(data['year'] ?? data['Year']);
    final prog = (data['Program/Series'] ?? data['Program / Series'] ?? data['program_series'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
    final theme = (data['Theme/Subject'] ?? data['theme_subject'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
    final title = (data['title'] ?? data['name'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
    final cond = <Object?>[
      data['Condition'], data['condition'],
      data['Variety'], data['variety'],
      data['Strike Type'], data['strike_type'],
      data['Denomination'], data['denomination'],
    ].where((v) => v != null).map((v) => v.toString().toLowerCase()).join(' ');
    final combined = '$prog $theme $title $cond';
    if (year == '2026' &&
        RegExp(r'\b(american|silver)\s+eagle\b').hasMatch(combined) &&
        !RegExp(r'\b(gold|platinum|palladium)\b').hasMatch(combined)) {
      return '26EA';
    }
    if (year == '2026') {
      if (combined.contains('morgan') && combined.contains('enhanced')) return '26XE';
      if (combined.contains('morgan') && combined.contains('proof')) return '26XF';
      if (combined.contains('peace') && combined.contains('reverse proof')) return '26XL';
      if (combined.contains('peace') && combined.contains('uncirculated')) return '26XH';
      if (combined.contains('uncirculated') && combined.contains('set')) return '26RJ';
      if (combined.contains('american innovation')) return '26GC';
      if (combined.contains('trump') || combined.contains('semiquincentennial president')) {
        return '26SQRP';
      }
      if (combined.contains('silver proof set')) return '26RH';
      if (combined.contains('clad proof set') ||
          (combined.contains('proof set') && !combined.contains('silver'))) {
        return '26RG';
      }
    }
    if (year == '2023') {
      if (combined.contains('silver proof set')) return '23RH';
      if (combined.contains('proof set')) return '23RG';
    }
    return (data['mint_product_id'] ??
            data['sku'] ??
            data['product_code'] ??
            data['Retailer Item No.'] ??
            '')
        .toString()
        .trim()
        .toUpperCase();
  }

  static CanonicalMintAssetPair? resolveFromMap(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    // MF-B Guard: Non-destructive preservation of user-uploaded / microscope photos.
    // If doc contains user uploads (e.g. Row 10 Eric's microscope photos), return null.
    if (hasUserUploadedPhotos(data)) {
      return null;
    }
    final sku = extractSku(data);
    // Intentionally excludes `Personal Notes` so neighbor-coin notes in live Firestore
    // never hijack ATB/State quarter identification.
    final primaryTitle = <String>[
      (data['title'] ?? '').toString().trim(),
      (data['name'] ?? '').toString().trim(),
      (data['Theme/Subject'] ?? '').toString().trim(),
      (data['theme_subject'] ?? '').toString().trim(),
      (data['Program / Series'] ?? data['Program/Series'] ?? '').toString().trim(),
      (data['program_series'] ?? '').toString().trim(),
      (data['description'] ?? '').toString().trim(),
    ].where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).join(' ');

    final year = _normYear(data['year'] ?? data['Year']);
    final mintMark =
        (data['mint_mark'] ?? data['mint'] ?? data['Mint Mark'] ?? '').toString().trim();
    final denom =
        (data['denomination'] ?? data['Denomination'] ?? '').toString().trim();
    final storedObv =
        (data['image_url_obverse'] ?? data['obverse_image'] ?? data['image_url'] ?? '')
            .toString()
            .trim();

    return resolve(
      sku: sku,
      title: primaryTitle,
      year: year,
      mintMark: mintMark,
      denomination: denom,
      storedObverseUrl: storedObv.isNotEmpty ? storedObv : null,
    );
  }

  static CanonicalMintAssetPair? resolve({
    String? sku,
    String? title,
    String? year,
    String? mintMark,
    String? denomination,
    String? storedObverseUrl,
  }) {
    final cleanTitle = (title ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    final cleanYear = _normYear(year);
    final rawMint = (mintMark ?? '').trim().toUpperCase();
    final cleanDenom = (denomination ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    final cleanMint = rawMint.contains('WEST POINT') || rawMint == 'W'
        ? 'W'
        : (rawMint.contains('S')
            ? 'S'
            : (rawMint.contains('D')
                ? 'D'
                : (rawMint.contains('P') ? 'P' : rawMint)));

    final rawSku = (sku ?? '').trim().toUpperCase();
    final cleanSku = rawSku.isNotEmpty
        ? rawSku
        : extractSku(<String, dynamic>{
            'year': cleanYear,
            'title': cleanTitle,
            'denomination': cleanDenom,
            'mint_mark': cleanMint,
          });

    // 1. Exact / Inferred SKU Match
    switch (cleanSku) {
      case '26EA':
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '26EA',
          obverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26EA_image_1.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26EA_image_2.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26ea_a.webp',
          fallbackReverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26ea_b.webp',
          isSet: false,
          isGenuine2026: true,
        );
      case '26XE':
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '26XE',
          obverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XE_image_2.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XE_image_3.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26xe_a.webp',
          fallbackReverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26xe_b.webp',
          isSet: false,
          isRepresentativePreview: true,
        );
      case '26XL':
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '26XL',
          obverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XL_image_3.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XL_image_2.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26xl___a.webp',
          fallbackReverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XL_image_1.jpg',
          isSet: false,
          isRepresentativePreview: true,
        );
      case '26XH':
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '26XH',
          obverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XH_image_2.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XH_image_3.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XH_image_1.jpg',
          fallbackReverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26xh_image_3.jpg',
          isSet: false,
          isRepresentativePreview: true,
        );
      case '26XF':
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '26XF',
          obverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XF_image_1.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XF_image_2.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XE_image_2.jpg',
          fallbackReverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26XE_image_3.jpg',
          isSet: false,
          isRepresentativePreview: true,
        );
      case '26RJ':
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '26RJ',
          obverseUrl:
              '$_base/user_contributed/coin_sets/uncirculated-coin-set-2026/26rj_e.jpg',
          reverseUrl:
              '$_base/user_contributed/coin_sets/uncirculated-coin-set-2026/26rj_b.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26RJ_image_1.jpg',
          fallbackReverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26RJ_image_2.jpg',
          isSet: true,
          isGenuine2026: true,
        );
      case '26GC':
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '26GC',
          obverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26GC_image_1.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26gc_b.webp',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_base/2026_series/2026_american_innovation_obverse.jpg',
          fallbackReverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26GC_image_2.jpg',
          isSet: true,
          isGenuine2026: true,
        );
      case '26SQRP':
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '26SQRP',
          obverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/MASTER_SEMIQDJT_image_1.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26sqrp_b.webp',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/MASTER_SEMIQDJT_image_2.jpg',
          fallbackReverseUrl: null,
          isSet: true,
          isGenuine2026: true,
        );
      case '26RG':
      case '23RG':
        return CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: cleanSku,
          obverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26RG_image_1.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26rg_b.webp',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26RG_image_2.jpg',
          fallbackReverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26RG_image_3.jpg',
          isSet: true,
          isRepresentativePreview: true,
        );
      case '26RH':
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '26RH',
          obverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26RH_image_1.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26RH_image_2.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2026_products/26RH_image_3.jpg',
          fallbackReverseUrl: null,
          isSet: true,
          isRepresentativePreview: true,
        );
      case '23RH':
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '23RH',
          obverseUrl:
              '$_base/2023_series/2023_american_women_quarters_five_honorees_composite.jpg',
          reverseUrl:
              '$_base/2023_series/2023_american_innovation_four_states_composite.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2023_products/23rh_b.webp',
          fallbackReverseUrl: '$_mintBase/2023_products/23rh_c.webp',
          isSet: true,
        );
    }

    // 2. 2026 Semiquincentennial Individual Coin Match (Rows 08ΓÇô11)
    if (cleanYear == '2026') {
      if (cleanTitle.contains('roosevelt') ||
          cleanTitle.contains('dime') ||
          cleanDenom.contains('10c') ||
          cleanDenom.contains('dime')) {
        final effMint = (cleanMint.isEmpty || cleanMint == 'P') ? 'P' : cleanMint;
        final obvFile = effMint == 'D'
            ? 'SemiQ-Dime-Obverse-Unc-D.jpg'
            : 'SemiQ-Dime-Obverse-Unc-P.jpg';
        return CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '2026-DIME-$effMint',
          obverseUrl: '$_base/bulk_programs/dime/$obvFile',
          reverseUrl: '$_base/bulk_programs/dime/SemiQ-Dime-Reverse-Unc.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: null,
          fallbackReverseUrl: null,
          isGenuine2026: true,
        );
      }
      final isQuarter = RegExp(r'\b(25c|25┬ó|quarter)\b').hasMatch(cleanDenom) ||
          RegExp(r'\bquarter\b').hasMatch(cleanTitle);
      final isHalf = RegExp(r'\b(50c|50┬ó|half)\b').hasMatch(cleanDenom) ||
          RegExp(r'\b(kennedy|half dollar)\b').hasMatch(cleanTitle);
      if (cleanTitle.contains('kennedy') ||
          cleanTitle.contains('half dollar') ||
          cleanDenom.contains('50c') ||
          cleanDenom.contains('half') ||
          (cleanTitle.contains('independence') && !isQuarter)) {
        final effMint = (cleanMint.isEmpty || cleanMint == 'P') ? 'P' : cleanMint;
        final obvUrl = effMint == 'D'
            ? '$_base/bulk_programs/half_dollar/SemiQ-Half-Dollar-Obverse-Unc-D.jpg'
            : '$_base/bulk_programs/us_mint_manual/highres_scrape/SemiQ-Half-Dollar-Obverse-Unc-P.jpg';
        return CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '2026-HALF-$effMint',
          obverseUrl: obvUrl,
          reverseUrl:
              '$_base/bulk_programs/half_dollar/SemiQ-Half-Dollar-Reverse-Unc.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: effMint == 'P'
              ? '$_base/2026_series/2026_half_dollar_enduring_liberty_obverse.jpg'
              : '$_base/bulk_programs/us_mint_manual/highres_scrape/SemiQ-Half-Dollar-Obverse-Unc-P.jpg',
          fallbackReverseUrl: null,
          isGenuine2026: true,
        );
      }
      if (cleanTitle.contains('mayflower')) {
        final effMint = (cleanMint.isEmpty || cleanMint == 'P') ? 'P' : cleanMint;
        final obvUrl = effMint == 'D'
            ? '$_base/bulk_programs/us_mint_manual/highres_scrape/SemiQ-Mayflower-Obverse-Unc-D.jpg'
            : '$_base/2026_series/2026_quarter_dollar_mayflower_compact_obverse.jpg';
        return CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '2026-MAYFLOWER-$effMint',
          obverseUrl: obvUrl,
          reverseUrl:
              '$_base/2026_series/2026_quarter_dollar_mayflower_compact_reverse.jpg',
          fallbackObverseUrl: effMint == 'D'
              ? '$_base/2026_series/2026_quarter_dollar_mayflower_compact_obverse.jpg'
              : null,
          fallbackReverseUrl: null,
          isGenuine2026: true,
        );
      }
      // 2026 Semiquincentennial quarters (Declaration, Gettysburg, Revolutionary War, Constitution)
      const qtrs = <String, String>{
        'declaration of independence': 'declaration_of_independence',
        'gettysburg': 'gettysburg_address',
        'revolutionary war': 'revolutionary_war',
        'constitution': 'us_constitution',
      };
      for (final e in qtrs.entries) {
        if (cleanTitle.contains(e.key) && (isQuarter || !isHalf)) {
          final effMint = (cleanMint.isEmpty || cleanMint == 'P') ? 'P' : cleanMint;
          return CanonicalMintAssetPair(
            sku: '2026-QTR-${e.value.toUpperCase()}-$effMint',
            obverseUrl: '$_base/2026_series/2026_quarter_dollar_${e.value}_obverse.jpg',
            reverseUrl: '$_base/2026_series/2026_quarter_dollar_${e.value}_reverse.jpg',
            isGenuine2026: true,
          );
        }
      }
      // 2026 Native American $1 (Polly Cooper)
      if (cleanTitle.contains('polly cooper') ||
          (RegExp(r'\bnative american\b').hasMatch(cleanTitle) &&
              (cleanDenom.contains('1') || cleanTitle.contains('dollar')))) {
        return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '2026-NATIVE-POLLY',
          obverseUrl: '$_base/2026_series/2026_native_american_dollar_polly_cooper_obverse.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_base/2026_series/2026_native_american_dollar_polly_cooper_reverse.jpg',
          isGenuine2026: true,
        );
      }
      // MF-1: cent and nickel, whole word or denomination field only. NEVER substring 'cent'.
      final isCent = RegExp(r'\b(1c|1┬ó|one cent|cent|penny)\b').hasMatch(cleanDenom) ||
          RegExp(r'\b(lincoln cent|one cent|penny)\b').hasMatch(cleanTitle);
      final isNickel = RegExp(r'\b(5c|5┬ó|five cents?|nickel)\b').hasMatch(cleanDenom) ||
          RegExp(r'\b(jefferson nickel|nickel|five cents?)\b').hasMatch(cleanTitle);
      if (isCent && !isQuarter && !isHalf) {
        final effMint = cleanMint == 'D' ? 'D' : 'P';
        return CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '2026-CENT-$effMint',
          obverseUrl: effMint == 'D'
              ? '$_base/bulk_programs/us_mint_manual/highres_scrape/SemiQ-Penny-Obverse-Unc-D.jpg'
              : '$_base/bulk_programs/penny/SemiQ-Penny-Obverse-Unc-P.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_base/bulk_programs/penny/SemiQ-Penny-Reverse-Unc.jpg',
          isGenuine2026: true,
        );
      }
      if (isNickel && !isQuarter && !isHalf) {
        final effMint = cleanMint == 'D' ? 'D' : 'P';
        return CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: '2026-NICKEL-$effMint',
          obverseUrl: '$_base/bulk_programs/nickel/SemiQ-Nickel-Obverse-Unc-$effMint.jpg',
          reverseUrl: '$_base/bulk_programs/nickel/SemiQ-Nickel-Reverse-Unc.jpg',
          isGenuine2026: true,
        );
      }
    }

    // 3. 2023 American Women Quarters (Synthetic Tracker B26-14..B26-18)
    if (cleanYear == '2023') {
      const awqObv =
          '$_base/2023_series/2023_american_women_quarters_shared_obverse.jpg';
      const awqFallbackObv =
          '$_base/2023_series/2023_american_women_quarters_shared_obverse.png';
      const awqRevs = <String, String>{
        'bessie coleman':
            '2023_american_women_quarters_bessie_coleman_reverse.jpg',
        'edith kanaka':
            '2023_american_women_quarters_edith_kanakaole_reverse.jpg',
        'kanakaole':
            '2023_american_women_quarters_edith_kanakaole_reverse.jpg',
        'eleanor roosevelt':
            '2023_american_women_quarters_eleanor_roosevelt_reverse.jpg',
        'jovita idar': '2023_american_women_quarters_jovita_idar_reverse.jpg',
        'maria tallchief':
            '2023_american_women_quarters_maria_tallchief_reverse.jpg',
      };
      for (final entry in awqRevs.entries) {
        if (cleanTitle.contains(entry.key)) {
          final revUrl = '$_base/2023_series/${entry.value}';
          return CanonicalMintAssetPair(
            sku: '2023-AWQ-${entry.key.split(' ').first.toUpperCase()}',
            obverseUrl: awqObv,
            reverseUrl: revUrl,
            fallbackObverseUrl: awqFallbackObv,
            fallbackReverseUrl: revUrl.replaceAll('.jpg', '.png'),
          );
        }
      }
    }

    // 4. 2005-D Kansas State Quarter (Row 12) - Strict && guard per MF8
    if (cleanYear == '2005' &&
        cleanMint == 'D' &&
        cleanTitle.contains('kansas') &&
        (cleanDenom.contains('quarter') ||
            cleanDenom.contains('25c') ||
            cleanTitle.contains('quarter'))) {
      return const CanonicalMintAssetPair(
        sku: '2005-SQ-KS-D',
        obverseUrl:
            '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/50_State_Quarters/50_State_and_Territories_quarter_obverse__28Denver_29.jpg',
        reverseUrl:
            '$_base/bulk_programs/50_state_quarters/2005-50-state-quarters-coin-kansas-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
        fallbackObverseUrl:
            '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/50_State_Quarters/50_State_and_Territories_quarter_obverse__28Philadelphia_29.jpg',
        fallbackReverseUrl:
            '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/50_State_Quarters/2005_United_States_quarters/50_State_quarter__Kansas_/Kansas_quarter_2C_reverse_side_2C_2005.jpg',
      );
    }

    // 5. 1962 Lincoln Cent (Row 18) - MF9: Obverse kept as stored; reverse replaced with Lincoln Memorial reverse
    if (cleanYear == '1962' &&
        (cleanTitle.contains('lincoln') ||
            cleanTitle.contains('cent') ||
            cleanDenom.contains('1c') ||
            cleanDenom.contains('cent'))) {
      return CanonicalMintAssetPair(
        sku: '1962-LINCOLN-1C',
        obverseUrl: storedObverseUrl ??
            '$_wikiBase/Coins__NNC_/NNC-US-1909-1C-Lincoln_Cent__28wheat_29_LEFT.jpg',
        reverseUrl:
            '$_wikiBase/United_States_cents/Lincoln_cents/Lincoln_Memorial_cent/United_States_penny_2C_reverse.jpg',
        fallbackObverseUrl:
            '$_wikiBase/United_States_cents/Obverses_of_United_States_cents/2002_Penny_Proof_Obv.png',
        fallbackReverseUrl: null,
      );
    }

    // 6. America the Beautiful (ATB) Quarters (Live Firestore Rows 13ΓÇô17, 19ΓÇô24 + Synthetic Tracker B26-25..B26-30)
    const atbRevs = <String, String>{
      'american memorial':
          '2019-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-american-memorial-park-northern-mariana-islands-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'salt river':
          '2020-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-salt-river-bay-us-virgin-islands-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'mount rushmore':
          '2013-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-mount-rushmore-south-dakota-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'cumberland island':
          '2018-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-cumberland-island-georgia-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'block island':
          '2018-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-block-island-rhode-island-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'san antonio':
          '2019-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-san-antonio-missions-texas-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'effigy mounds':
          '2017-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-effigy-mounds-iowa-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'frederick douglass':
          '2017-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-frederick-douglass-district-of-columbia-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'yellowstone':
          '2010-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-yellowstone-wyoming-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'george rogers clark':
          '2017-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-george-rogers-clark-indiana-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'ozark':
          '2017-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-ozark-riverways-missouri-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'white mountain':
          '2013-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-white-mountain-new-hampshire-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'mount hood':
          '2010-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-mount-hood-oregon-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'marsh-billings':
          '2020-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-marsh-billings-rockefeller-vermont-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'tallgrass':
          '2020-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-tallgrass-prairie-kansas-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
      'weir farm':
          '2020-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-weir-farm-connecticut-uncirculated-reverse.jpg',
    };

    const atbObvByYearMint = <String, String>{
      '2013-P':
          '$_base/atb_quarters/2013-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-uncirculated-obverse-p.jpg',
      '2013-D':
          '$_base/atb_quarters/2013-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-uncirculated-obverse-d.jpg',
      '2013-S':
          '$_base/atb_quarters/2013-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-proof-obverse-s.jpg',
      '2018-P':
          '$_base/atb_quarters/2018-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-uncirculated-obverse-philadelphia.jpg',
      '2018-D':
          '$_base/atb_quarters/2018-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-uncirculated-obverse-denver.jpg',
      '2019-P':
          '$_base/atb_quarters/2019-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-uncirculated-obverse-philadelphia.jpg',
      '2019-D':
          '$_base/atb_quarters/2019-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-uncirculated-obverse-denver.jpg',
      '2020-P':
          '$_base/atb_quarters/2020-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-uncirculated-obverse-philadelphia.jpg',
      '2020-D':
          '$_base/atb_quarters/2020-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-uncirculated-obverse-denver.jpg',
    };

    for (final entry in atbRevs.entries) {
      if (cleanTitle.contains(entry.key)) {
        final revUrl = '$_base/atb_quarters/${entry.value}';
        final exactObv = atbObvByYearMint['$cleanYear-$cleanMint'];
        late final String obvUrl;
        late final String fallbackObv;
        if (exactObv != null) {
          obvUrl = exactObv;
          fallbackObv =
              '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/America_the_Beautiful_Quarters/America_the_Beautiful_quarter_2C_obverse.jpg';
        } else if (cleanMint == 'S') {
          obvUrl =
              '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/50_State_Quarters/50_State_and_Territories_quarter_proof_obverse__28San_Francisco_29.jpg';
          fallbackObv =
              '$_base/atb_quarters/2014-america-the-beautiful-quarters-coin-proof-obverse-s.jpg';
        } else if (cleanMint == 'D') {
          obvUrl =
              '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/America_the_Beautiful_Quarters/America_the_Beautiful_quarter_2C_obverse.jpg';
          fallbackObv =
              '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/50_State_Quarters/50_State_and_Territories_quarter_obverse__28Denver_29.jpg';
        } else if (cleanMint == 'W') {
          // MF8: Do not assign a P-mint obverse to a W-mint coin; use undated ATB obverse
          obvUrl =
              '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/America_the_Beautiful_Quarters/America_the_Beautiful_quarter_2C_obverse.jpg';
          fallbackObv =
              '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/America_the_Beautiful_quarter_obverse__28Philadeplhia_29.jpg';
        } else {
          // MF8: Undated Philadelphia ATB obverse (no conflicting year in filename)
          obvUrl =
              '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/America_the_Beautiful_quarter_obverse__28Philadeplhia_29.jpg';
          fallbackObv =
              '$_wikiBase/United_States_quarters/Washington_quarter/America_the_Beautiful_Quarters/America_the_Beautiful_quarter_2C_obverse.jpg';
        }
        return CanonicalMintAssetPair(
          sku: 'ATB-$cleanYear-$cleanMint',
          obverseUrl: obvUrl,
          reverseUrl: revUrl,
          fallbackObverseUrl: fallbackObv,
          fallbackReverseUrl: null,
        );
      }
    }

    return null;
  }
}
### FILE: numista_mobile/lib/services/coin_image_service.dart
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart' show debugPrint, visibleForTesting;
import 'canonical_mint_asset_service.dart';

/// Looks up reference coin images from the Firestore coin_image_index collection,
/// which is built by build_image_index.py from the GCS buckets:
///   - gs://us_mint_coin_images        (Tier 1 ΓÇö US Mint official images)
///   - gs://numista-reference-library  (Tier 2ΓÇô5 ΓÇö reference + Wikimedia)
///
/// Key format in Firestore: {year}[_{mint}][_{subject}]_{program}_{side}
/// where subject is the state/president/woman slug for series coins.
///
/// Returns null for both obverse and reverse if no image is found ΓÇö the caller
/// should fall back to "No photo yet" rather than showing an error.

class CoinImageService {
  static const _collection = 'coin_image_index';

  /// Programs that have per-design subjects (state, president, woman, etc.)
  /// Matches SUBJECT_PROGRAMS in build_image_index.py.
  static const _subjectPrograms = {
    '50-state-quarters',
    'presidential-dollars',
    'american-women-quarters',
    'america-the-beautiful',
    'american-innovation',
    'native-american-dollar',
    'commemorative',
    'uncirculated-sets',
    'semiquincentennial',
    // Generic programs ΓÇö subject resolution still useful for fallback
    'dollar',
    'quarter',
  };

  /// Maps subject names (states, presidents, women) to canonical slugs.
  /// Mirrors STATE_SLUG_MAP in build_image_index.py.
  static const _subjectSlugMap = <String, String>{
    // 50 US States
    'alabama': 'alabama', 'alaska': 'alaska', 'arizona': 'arizona',
    'arkansas': 'arkansas', 'california': 'california', 'colorado': 'colorado',
    'connecticut': 'connecticut', 'delaware': 'delaware', 'florida': 'florida',
    'georgia': 'georgia', 'hawaii': 'hawaii', 'idaho': 'idaho',
    'illinois': 'illinois', 'indiana': 'indiana', 'iowa': 'iowa',
    'kansas': 'kansas', 'kentucky': 'kentucky', 'louisiana': 'louisiana',
    'maine': 'maine', 'maryland': 'maryland', 'massachusetts': 'massachusetts',
    'michigan': 'michigan', 'minnesota': 'minnesota', 'mississippi': 'mississippi',
    'missouri': 'missouri', 'montana': 'montana', 'nebraska': 'nebraska',
    'nevada': 'nevada', 'new hampshire': 'new-hampshire', 'new jersey': 'new-jersey',
    'new mexico': 'new-mexico', 'new york': 'new-york',
    'north carolina': 'north-carolina', 'north dakota': 'north-dakota',
    'ohio': 'ohio', 'oklahoma': 'oklahoma', 'oregon': 'oregon',
    'pennsylvania': 'pennsylvania', 'rhode island': 'rhode-island',
    'south carolina': 'south-carolina', 'south dakota': 'south-dakota',
    'tennessee': 'tennessee', 'texas': 'texas', 'utah': 'utah',
    'vermont': 'vermont', 'virginia': 'virginia', 'washington': 'washington',
    'west virginia': 'west-virginia', 'wisconsin': 'wisconsin', 'wyoming': 'wyoming',
    // Territories
    'puerto rico': 'puerto-rico', 'guam': 'guam',
    'us virgin islands': 'us-virgin-islands',
    'american samoa': 'american-samoa',
    'northern mariana': 'northern-mariana-islands',
    'district of columbia': 'district-of-columbia',
    // ATB parks
    'yellowstone': 'yellowstone', 'grand canyon': 'grand-canyon',
    'yosemite': 'yosemite', 'gettysburg': 'gettysburg',
    'hot springs': 'hot-springs', 'mount hood': 'mount-hood',
    'glacier': 'glacier', 'olympic': 'olympic',
    // ATB park name ΓåÆ state/territory slug (2010ΓÇô2021 complete program)
    // Note: coin_image_index keys images by state (from filename), not park name.
    // 2010
    'hot springs national park': 'arkansas',
    'yellowstone national park': 'wyoming',
    'yosemite national park': 'california',
    'grand canyon national park': 'arizona',
    'mount hood national forest': 'oregon',
    // 2011
    'gettysburg national military park': 'pennsylvania',
    'glacier national park': 'montana',
    'olympic national park': 'washington',
    'vicksburg national military park': 'mississippi',
    'chickasaw national recreation area': 'oklahoma',
    // 2012
    'el yunque national forest': 'puerto-rico',
    'chaco culture national historical park': 'new-mexico',
    'acadia national park': 'maine',
    'hawaii volcanoes national park': 'hawaii',
    'denali national park': 'alaska',
    // 2013
    'white mountain national forest': 'new-hampshire',
    "perry's victory and international peace memorial": 'ohio',
    'great basin national park': 'nevada',
    'fort mchenry national monument': 'maryland',
    'mount rushmore national memorial': 'south-dakota',
    // 2014
    'great smoky mountains national park': 'tennessee',
